Third-party crates in billie-translator

Written by tools/crate_notices.py from runtime/rust/Cargo.toml and its lockfile; do not edit.
Each crate below is compiled into the binary or used to build it. Its licence
files follow, verbatim; a text several crates ship is given once.

goat-transformers
Copyright 2025-2026 U.S. Federal Government (in countries where recognized)

Portions of this work written by U.S. Federal government employees within the
scope of their federal employment are not subject to copyright protection in the
United States (17 U.S.C. 105) and are in the public domain there. In countries
where copyright protection is available, those portions are released under the
Apache License, Version 2.0. Merged contributions from private contributors are
released under the same License. See INTENT.md.

This product is licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (see LICENSE at
the root of this repository).

The engines use only the standard library of their language. No third-party
code is included in this repository.

The packs under packs/ and the model modules under model/ were written from the
third-party sources below. The source files are not included. A pack records
field names, field numbers, positions, types, units and code values. Each
pack's PROVENANCE.md and the `source` lines of its pack.def state the exact
version, the SHA-256 of each file read and the license statement of the source.
The license of each source is given here as that source states it.

The packs named conformance*, demo-* and the module probe describe synthetic
formats written for this project and have no external source.

Pack a-gra; module agra
  Autonomy Government Reference Architecture (A-GRA) schema 6.0a (version
  006.0.ASK), U.S. Air Force. https://github.com/open-arsenal/a-gra
  File header: Distribution Statement A. Approved for public release:
  distribution is unlimited.

Pack adsb-df18, and the type code 28 subtype 2 kinds of pack adsb; module adsb
  pyModeS, https://github.com/junzis/pyModeS, GNU General Public License,
  Version 3. Field layouts restated from its decoders, and published test
  messages with their decoded values; no code.

Pack adsb; module adsb
  Junzi Sun, "The 1090 Megahertz Riddle: A Guide to Decoding Mode S and ADS-B
  Signals", second edition, TU Delft OPEN Publishing.
  https://mode-s.org/1090mhz/  Copyright 2021 Junzi Sun. CC BY-NC-SA 4.0.
  Field positions, widths, code meanings and five example messages; no text,
  table or figure.
  pyModeS, https://github.com/junzis/pyModeS  GNU General Public License,
  Version 3. Published test messages and their decoded values; no code.

  https://gitlab.com/gpsd/gpsd at commit c59fc6de. Compilation copyright is
  held by the GPSD project. BSD-2-Clause (gpsd COPYING). Layouts restated; no
  text, table or example message.

Packs ais, ais-binary; modules ais, aisbin
  gpsd, "AIVDM/AIVDO protocol decoding", v1.58, Eric S. Raymond, file
  www/AIVDM.adoc of https://gitlab.com/gpsd/gpsd at commit
  c59fc6de479d06620c8b2ba395b728e10a71527b. Compilation copyright is held by
  the GPSD project. BSD-2-Clause (gpsd COPYING). Bit positions, widths, units
  and code meanings only; no text, table or example message.

  trademark statements in full:

    APRS® is a registered trademark of Bob Bruninga.

  https://github.com/semuconsulting/pyunigps v1.0.1. BSD 3-Clause License,
  Copyright (c) 2020 semuadin (Steve Smith) (the upstream spelling). Message
  names and IDs, field names, types, scales and bit widths only; read from the
  definition files, which are not run.

  U.S. public domain.

# The flags of every licence a pack or a model module may carry (REUSE.toml
# gives each pack its one licence). No pack states flags itself; everything
# a tool decides about use is derived from this table.
#
#   commercial    "yes" | "no" | "unknown": may be used in a commercial product
#   share_alike   an adaptation must carry the same licence
#   attribution   a notice must ship with it (its text is in NOTICE)
#   field_of_use  uses the licence prohibits; empty when none
#   distribution  "public", or "controlled" (private overlay only)
#
# The profiles (tools/licences.py, spec/engine-spec.md Version 0.29):
#   open           commercial "yes", no share-alike, no field of use (the default)
#   open+copyleft  open, and share-alike packs
#   everything     every public pack: also non-commercial, unknown and field-of-use
# A pack's profile is the narrowest profile that holds its own licence and the
# licence of every model module it uses.

[licences."Apache-2.0"]
name = "Apache License 2.0"
url = "https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0"
commercial = "yes"
share_alike = false
attribution = true
field_of_use = []
distribution = "public"

[licences."CC-BY-NC-SA-4.0"]
name = "Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial-ShareAlike 4.0 International"
url = "https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-nc-sa/4.0/"
commercial = "no"
share_alike = true
attribution = true
field_of_use = []
distribution = "public"

[licences."CC-BY-SA-4.0"]
name = "Creative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 4.0 International"
url = "https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/4.0/"
commercial = "yes"
share_alike = true
attribution = true
field_of_use = []
distribution = "public"

[licences."CC-BY-SA-2.0"]
name = "Creative Commons Attribution-ShareAlike 2.0 Generic"
url = "https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by-sa/2.0/"

attribution = true

distribution = "public"

field_of_use = []
distribution = "public"
